schedule(static)\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {\n        const size_t i = offset + k;\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[k].spot *= factor;\n        options[k].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));","originalFile":"// Hybrid-parallel Black-Scholes benchmark:\n//   - MPI: options are block-distributed across ranks (one rank per GPU),\n//     results are gathered on rank 0 for output/validation.\n//   - CUDA: each rank prices the bulk of its local block on its GPU.\n//   - OpenMP: parallel option generation on the host, plus a CPU share of the\n//     pricing that runs concurrently with the asynchronous GPU work.\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                          \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: grid-stride loop pricing one option per work item\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(const OptionInput* __restrict__ options,\n                                   double* __restrict__ results,\n                                   const size_t n) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n         i < n; i += stride) {\n        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate options for global indices [offset, offset + count) by scaling the\n// test set; identical values to the original serial generation for any split.\nvoid generateOptionsRange(OptionInput* options, const size_t offset, const size_t count) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {\n        const size_t i = offset + k;\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[k] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[k].spot *= factor;\n        options[k].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n The benchmark now runs as an unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid, with only `black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp` and `black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt` modified and the executable still named `black_scholes`.\n\n**Parallelization design**\n- **MPI**: the global option range is block-distributed across ranks; each rank binds to a GPU by node-local rank (`MPI_Comm_split_type` with `MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED`, then `nodeRank % deviceCount`), so on a cluster you run one rank per GPU. Results are gathered on rank 0 with `MPI_Gatherv` for output and validation, and the reported time is the slowest rank's (`MPI_Reduce` MAX after a barrier), which is the honest cluster-wide figure.\n- **CUDA**: each rank prices the bulk (31/32) of its block with a grid-stride double-precision kernel using pinned host buffers and an async H2D → kernel → D2H pipeline on a stream. A warmup launch outside the timed region absorbs lazy module loading.\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes option generation on the host and computes the remaining 1/32 CPU share of each rank's block concurrently with the asynchronous GPU work.\n\n**Correctness**: option generation is done per global index, so values are identical to the serial code for any rank count. I rebuilt the original serial program in /tmp as a reference: sums agree to 16 significant digits and samples are bit-identical or within 1–2 ulps (the GPU's `erf` differs from glibc's in the last ulp, so the FNV hash of raw bits differs — unavoidable for a GPU port).
